A single GAP (a novel op with no CPU kernel, or a contract mismatch) exits the throughput leg and routes that op to the cpu-optimizer (new-kernel) leg. This gate is what makes the throughput-thesis claim honest — it refuses to dense-approximate a novel op just to make the demo pass.
